# Activity (July 19 → July 20) — **The Vienna Hackathon's first full interop day — five relays under simultaneous test, a live cross-implementation matrix, and the code to match.** July 19 (Hackathon Day 1, Sunday) turned `#moq` into a live interop channel with **five relays exercised at once**: [[moxygen|Moxygen]] (`fb.mvfst.net:9448/moq-relay`), [[imquic]] (`lminiero.it:9000`, draft-19), [[yu-you|Nokia]] (`moqt://moqt.nokiaresearch.com:4443/moq`, draft-18), [[mike-english|Cloudflare]] (`draft-18-interop.cloudflare.mediaoverquic.com:443`), and [[luke-curley|Luke Curley]]'s `cdn.moq.pro/anon`. **Jordi Cenzano's (Meta) `moq-encoder-player` matrix**: Moxygen, imquic, and Nokia all did **full video+audio**; **Cloudflare failed on PUBLISH** and **`cdn.moq.pro` failed on subscribe**. Two spec-interpretation findings surfaced on the floor: a **rendezvous-timeout semantics** debate (afrind ↔ Yu You, Nokia patched mid-Hackathon) and **`cdn.moq.pro`'s IETF path being *"nowhere near as tested"*** — plus [[luke-curley|Luke Curley]]'s statement that Hang **will likely never implement the draft-19 filters** (*"complicates billing"*), reinforcing that filters are the ecosystem's problem child (see also ianswett's new [transport #1816](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/issues/1816) *"Range filters only filter integer Properties"* and imquic's partial support). The code matched the floor: **[[imquic]] merged draft-19 to `main`** ([PR #32](https://github.com/meetecho/imquic/pull/32), +1124/−211), **[[moqtail]] ran a ~11-PR control-plane-restructure sprint** ([#292–#302](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/302)), **[[moq-rs|cloudflare/moq-rs]] landed SUBSCRIBE_NAMESPACE** ([PR #187](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/187), Manish) + a v0.7.23 release round and rebased its draft-18 Hackathon branch, **[[kota-yatagai|Kota Yatagai]]'s Moqtopus** added a +2007-line publisher/subscriber flow, and Giovanni Marzot ran an **[[aiomoqt]] v0.10.6** control-plane regression. On the IETF side, **IETF 126 Vienna sessions begin (Monday July 20, 12:00 UTC)** — [`agenda-126-moq-03`](https://datatracker.ietf.org/meeting/126/materials/agenda-126-moq) with **6 slide decks posted** (incl. "MOQT Updates Since IETF 125", LOC, Secure Objects, Auth Design Team, and a new performance/feedback-requirements deck) — the **weekly GitHub digest resumed** (July 19, first since July 12), Jordi Cenzano landed a **[loc #27](https://github.com/moq-wg/loc/pull/27)** TIMESTAMP-registry IANA fix, afrind opened **[transport #1817](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1817)** (moqt-URI host resolution), and the **Oct 14–15 Seattle in-person interim** was formally IESG-announced. Interop recovers **+2 to 134** (July-19 00:33, 319/134/185/0, at-target draft-18 171). No new draft revisions (all 07-06); no new [[moq-monthly|MoQ Monthly]] (#2, May 31); no open wiki issues.**

## The Vienna Hackathon's first full interop day — five relays, one live matrix

July 19 (Hackathon Day 1, Sunday) produced the window's richest hands-on interop, with participants asked to log results to the [ad-hoc interop wiki](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/wiki/ad-hoc-interop-reports#2026-07-ietf-126-vienna) so [[mike-english|Mike English]] can summarize them for **Friday's (July 24) interop report**. Five relays were under simultaneous cross-implementation test:

- **[[moxygen|Moxygen]]** — afrind's `fb.mvfst.net:9448/moq-relay`, plus an offer of a **fan-out performance test** (*"tell me how many subscribers you think you can handle for a given track size, and I will hit you"*).
- **[[imquic]]** — Miniero's `lminiero.it:9000`, now running draft-19.
- **[[yu-you|Nokia]]** — Yu You's `moqt://moqt.nokiaresearch.com:4443/moq` (draft-18), redeployed July 19 with fixes for redundant request_ids and subscriber-forced forwarding.
- **[[mike-english|Cloudflare]]** — `draft-18-interop.cloudflare.mediaoverquic.com:443`, which Mike briefly broke with a bad rebase, then restored to *"the initial draft-18 features + PUBLISH and SUBSCRIBE_NAMESPACE."*
- **[[luke-curley|Luke Curley]]'s Hang CDN** — `cdn.moq.pro/anon` (draft 14-19).

**Jordi Cenzano's (Meta) interop matrix** — using the [`moq-encoder-player`](https://github.com/facebookexperimental/moq-encoder-player) with the MOQ-MIv3 packager (migrating to [[moq-loc|LOC]]) — was the clearest single artifact of the day:

| Relay | Result |
|---|---|
| Moxygen | ✅ full video + audio |
| imquic (Miniero's) | ✅ full video + audio |
| Nokia | ✅ full video + audio |
| Cloudflare | ❌ failing on PUBLISH (*"seems NOT implemented"*) |
| `cdn.moq.pro/anon` | ❌ failing on subscribe (*"publisher not found"*) |

Other participants corroborated: [[kota-yatagai|Kota Yatagai]]'s **Moqtopus** (interopping *"on a train"*) verified SETUP/SUBSCRIBE/SUBSCRIBE_OK/REQUEST_ERROR against Moxygen and PUB_NS+SUBSCRIBE against Nokia (subgroup **and** datagram modes) but couldn't yet reach `cdn.moq.pro/anon`; [[suhas-nandakumar|Suhas Nandakumar]]'s draft-18 moq-web *"still working fine with all the relays I tested during London"*; and Giovanni Marzot's **[[aiomoqt]] v0.10.6** ran a Python control-plane regression with *"good results."* afrind's `openmoq/moqx` [`moq_decode.py`](https://github.com/openmoq/moqx/blob/main/tools/moq_decode.py) tool was in active use for wire-level debugging (he posted a REQUEST_ERROR frame decode that surfaced a string-underflow parse bug).

## Two design signals from the floor — filters and rendezvous timeout

The Hackathon surfaced two durable protocol signals that the automated runner would never have found:

- **Filters keep accumulating "no" votes.** [[luke-curley|Luke Curley]], asked by Miniero whether `cdn.moq.pro` supports filters: *"nope and I don't think I'll ever implement them… really complicates billing"* — a relay would have to *"charge based on the non-filtered amount."* This joins [[imquic]]'s partial filter support (`OBJECT_PROPERTY_FILTER` / `TRACK_PROPERTY_FILTER` ignored), the contested WG semantics ([moq-transport #1810](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/issues/1810), #1801), and **ianswett's new [#1816](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/issues/1816)** *"Range filters only filter integer Properties"* (opened July 19). The new draft-19 filters — the headline draft-18→19 delta — are proving the least-wanted, least-implemented part of the draft.
- **Rendezvous-timeout semantics are ambiguous.** afrind hit *"track not found"* subscribing through Nokia's relay to his own PUB_NS'd namespace. The root cause: Nokia's relay, with `RENDEZVOUS_TIMEOUT`=0 (default), *"won't try to do upstream subscriptions unless there's an active publisher for the given track."* afrind reads rendezvous as applying *only* when no PUBLISH_NAMESPACE is advertised at subscribe time (*"the relay should always try upstream subscriptions [when a namespace is advertised]"*). Yu You deployed a fix mid-Hackathon; afrind and Kota both confirmed **PUB_NS+SUBSCRIBE working** afterward. A concrete spec-interpretation gap in relay routing behavior, resolved on the floor.

Separately, the `cdn.moq.pro` subscribe failures (Miniero, Jordi, afrind) traced to Hang's IETF path being *"nowhere near as tested"* as its own clients: it sends **unsolicited `PUBLISH_NAMESPACE`** (a holdover for clients that don't implement `SUBSCRIBE_NAMESPACE`, which Luke plans to change to *require* SUBSCRIBE_NAMESPACE), and its recently-added QUIC `PATH` support may not be deployed. See [[interop-endpoints]], [[interop-runner]].

## The code matched the floor — imquic draft-19, moqtail's control-plane sprint, moq-rs SUBSCRIBE_NAMESPACE

The Hackathon drove a heavy code day across four implementations:

- **[[imquic]] merged draft-19 to `main`** — [PR #32](https://github.com/meetecho/imquic/pull/32) *"Add support for MoQT v19"* (Miniero, +1124/−211, merged July 19 09:28), folding the draft-19 build (previously running ahead of the repo) into `main`. This makes imquic the **first tracked-repo implementation with draft-19 in `main`**. Follow-up relay commits: *"Allow REQUEST_UPDATE to PUBLISH as a subscriber,"* FORWARD-related relay fixes, PUBLISH tweaks.
- **[[moqtail]] ran a ~11-PR control-plane-restructure sprint** over the weekend ([#292–#302](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/302), working down the RS-/CL-/RL- task list, issues #230–#242): request types moved onto **their own bidirectional streams** ([#297](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/297)), the **control plane onto a pair of uni streams** ([#294](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/294)), **PUBLISH_OK folded into REQUEST_OK + Track Properties** ([#299](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/299)), **MAX_REQUEST_ID / REQUESTS_BLOCKED removed** ([#298](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/298)), **Request ID removed from response messages** ([#302](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/302)), stream reset codes ([#300](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/300)), GREASE + reserved Property ranges ([#296](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/296)), `moqt://` as the unified selectable-transport scheme ([#295](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/295)), Mandatory Track Properties enforcement ([#293](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/293)), and delta-encoded FETCH Group/Object IDs ([#292](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/292)).
- **[[moq-rs|cloudflare/moq-rs]] landed SUBSCRIBE_NAMESPACE** — [PR #187](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/187) (Manish) merged to `main` July 19; `draft-18-dev` was rebased onto `main` the same day to prep the Hackathon relay, cut as `moq-relay-ietf` **v0.7.23** (+ moq-pub v0.9.1 / moq-sub v0.4.12 / moq-clock-ietf v0.6.18 / moq-test-client v0.1.10). This is the SUBSCRIBE_NAMESPACE feature Mike put live mid-Hackathon (though SUBSCRIBE-reaching-the-publisher was still gapped in testing).
- **[[kota-yatagai|Moqtopus]]** (Kota Yatagai) landed a +2007-line *"Publisher addition and subscriber flow change at IETF126"* commit — the PUBLISH+SUBSCRIBE_TRACK flow he said he'd add this week.
- **[[openmoq|moqx]]** — Giovanni Marzot's [PR #496](https://github.com/openmoq/moqx/pull/496) *"moq_decode: request_id cleanup"* (request_id left response messages in draft-18) aligned the shared decoder with draft-18.

[[moq-dev|moq-dev/moq]] had a lighter July-19 day (no release; a `moq-stats` tier-default removal [#2411](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2411), a demo download-stats fix [#2403](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2403), a signals abort-scope fix [#2402](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2402); OPEN Opus-output-sample-rate #2404 and unannounce-offline-broadcasts #2410). [[moxygen]] and [[quiche-moq|google/quiche]] (moqt) were quiet/infra-only. See [[imquic]], [[moqtail]], [[moq-rs]], [[moq-dev]].

Amid the draft-18-vs-19 activity, [[alan-frindell|Alan Frindell]] **posted a chair clarification: the official interop target is still draft-18** ("since there's been some confusion"), with -19 welcome for filter feedback. On the WG side, afrind **completed the IANA cross-document response** — opening "Early IANA feedback" tracking issues on the last three reviewed drafts ([[moq-loc|loc #26]], [[moq-secure-objects|secure-objects #90]], [[moq-privacy-pass|privacy-pass #25]]), so all five IANA-reviewed documents now have trackers ([[moq-transport|transport #1814]] + [[moq-msf|msf #191]] existed already). [[moq-dev|moq-dev/moq]] ran another busy July-18 day — **`moq-stats` crate extraction merged** ([#2380](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2380)), a big `net` refactor ([#2396](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2396)), moq-hls **recording cursors** ([#2389](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2389)) — and cut a fresh **moq-relay v0.13.7** train. ## The Vienna Hackathon goes live — first hands-on interop, and the first draft-19 relay

The IETF-126 Hackathon (Vienna, week of Sun July 20) started producing real interop on **July 18**, and Slack `#moq` — quiet-then-logistics through July 17 — turned into a live testing channel:

- **First draft-19 relay in the ecosystem.** [[lorenzo-miniero|Lorenzo Miniero]] deployed *"a first draft-19 version of my relay online at the usual spot"* ([[imquic]], `lminiero.it:9000`) and asked anyone with draft-19 support to test. The bulk of the draft-18 → -19 delta is the new **filters**: serialization/deserialization works and the basic filters function, but **`OBJECT_PROPERTY_FILTER` / `TRACK_PROPERTY_FILTER` are currently ignored** — Miniero has doubts about their intended semantics (echoing [moq-transport #1810](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/issues/1810) *"New section on filters hard to process"*). This is the wiki's first tracked draft-19 *interop endpoint*, the draft-19 code running ahead of the public `main` branch (PR #32 still in progress). See [[imquic]], [[interop-endpoints]].
- **Cloudflare draft-18 relay under live test.** [[mike-english|Mike English]] pointed testers at his **draft-18 single-instance relay** `draft-18-interop.cloudflare.mediaoverquic.com:443` (the runner's `moq-rs-draft-18`). [[yu-you|Yu You]] (Nokia) ran a basic v18 conformance client (`moq-test-client v0.1.0`): tests **1-4 pass** (setup-only, announce-only, publish-namespace-done, subscribe-error) but tests **5-7 fail** (announce-subscribe, object-vectors, subscribe-before-announce) — the relay rejects `PUBLISH` with `errCode=0x0 reason="not supported"`. Concrete evidence the Cloudflare draft-18 relay doesn't yet accept the upstream PUBLISH flow — exactly the kind of gap live Hackathon testing surfaces that the automated runner's endpoint pairings may not.
- **Hang 14-19 stack demoed end-to-end.** [[luke-curley|Luke Curley]] walked Jordi Cenzano (Meta) through the Hang public stack: `cdn.moq.pro/anon` supports draft **14-19**; the JS clients `moq.pub?relay=<host>` and `moq.watch?relay=<host>` and the Rust `moq-cli` (`cargo install moq-cli`) likewise; and `cdn.moq.pro` fronts **RTMP** (`rtmps://cdn.moq.pro:1935`), **SRT** (`srt://cdn.moq.pro:877`), and **WHEP** (`https://cdn.moq.pro/whep/…`) converters into/out of the same broadcast. See [[moq-dev]], [[interop-endpoints]].

In-person: Manish, Miniero, and Jordi Cenzano were in the Hackathon room July 18; Miniero was pulled to the NOC for part of the day; Kota Yatagai, afrind, and Yu You (Nokia) had said they'd arrive Sunday.

## Chair ruling: the interop target is still draft-18

The live draft-18/draft-19 mix prompted a clarification from the chair. [[alan-frindell|Alan Frindell]] (July 18): *"Since there's been some confusion, the official interop target for moq-transport is still **draft-18**. Intrepid souls are welcome to implement -19 (filter feedback is definitely valuable)."* This settles the question the wiki has flagged for two weeks — the runner has held draft-18 while moq-dev shipped -19 and imquic began -19 work. The reading: draft-18 stays the coordination baseline through Vienna, and -19 experimentation (especially the new filters, whose semantics are actively contested — see [moq-transport #1810](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/issues/1810), #1801) is encouraged precisely to shake out design issues before -19 becomes the target. See [[interop-runner]].

## IANA early review was a five-document sweep — the cross-draft registry punch-list

The transport-19 IANA early review logged last update (#1456083) was **one of five reviews Amanda Baber (IANA, via RT) filed in an eight-minute burst on the morning of July 16** (10:44–10:52 UTC). The four companion reviews:

- **loc-03 ([IANA #1456079])** — §6.1 references a nonexistent *"Property Headers"* registry (a *"Properties"* registry exists in transport with 0x06 already allocated); §6.2's *"Streaming Formats"* registry can't be found in the transport doc.
- **msf-01 ([IANA #1456080])** — §14.2 missing registry-group name / Change Controller / designated-expert guidance (RFC 8126); §14.3 references nonexistent registries with naming inconsistent vs transport-19; §14.4 needs the Private-Use range upper limit clarified.
- **privacy-pass-auth-03 ([IANA #1456081])** — supply the registry-group name and designated-expert guidance for two new registries (RFC 8126 §4.6).
- **secure-objects-01 ([IANA #1456082])** — §7.1 references a *"MoQ Object Properties"* registry, but transport-19 actually establishes *"MoQ Properties"* (fields Type/Name/Scope/Specification); type 0x02 already allocated.

The common thread across all five is a single defect class: registry names/references in the satellite drafts that don't line up with the registries transport-19 actually creates, plus missing RFC 8126 designated-expert/Change-Controller boilerplate. That makes the transport-19 review's *"reconcile allocations across loc/secure-objects/msf"* note IANA's **cross-document verdict** — the registry hygiene a cluster of documents needs before Last Call. [[will-law|Will Law]] opened the first document being worked down: **msf PRs [#192](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/pull/192)** *update normative references + IANA registries* (closes the [msf #191](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/issues/191) IANA tracker), **[#193](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/pull/193)** catalog-object publishing guidelines, **[#194](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/pull/194)** add a `data` reserved track role, **[#195](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/pull/195)** event-timeline JSON-example fixes (all OPEN, July 17). See [[moq-transport]], [[moq-msf]].

## Draft-18 convergence widens to a fifth codebase — google/quiche joins

[[quiche-moq|google/quiche]]'s MoQT module, quiet since July 8, landed **three substantial draft-18 migration commits July 17**:

- **`78341592` — move SUBSCRIBE to a dedicated bidirectional stream**: a large refactor introducing `MoqtSubscribeRequestStream` / `MoqtSubscribeResponseStream`; SUBSCRIBE / SUBSCRIBE_OK / UNSUBSCRIBE are removed from the control stream, the `MoqtUnsubscribe` message type is dropped, and the session **no longer owns subscriptions**.
- **`2f4ea0dd` — mass Moqt-class rename**: `SubscriptionPublisher`→`LivePublisher`, `SubscribeRemoteTrack`→`LiveSubscriber`, `RemoteTrack`→`ObjectSubscriber`, plus namespace/publish-stream renames.
- **`d106f181` — split SUBSCRIBE_NAMESPACE and SUBSCRIBE_TRACKS**: removes `SubscribeNamespaceOption`; adds stubs/parser/framer for SUBSCRIBE_TRACKS.

This is Google doing the same wire migration [[moqtail]] and [[moxygen]] did, from a very different C++ base. Together with moqtail (finishing its sprint), moxygen, imquic (#32 v19), and moq-dev (on draft-19), **five independent codebases are now on or converging toward the runner's draft-18 target**. moqtail closed its sprint's last two OPEN PRs — **[#290](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/290)** *add the FIRST_OBJECT bit to SUBGROUP_HEADER* (+213/−26, MERGED) and Kerem Bekmez's **[#291](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/291)** *moqtail-ts control-message-type table → draft-18* (MERGED). [[moxygen]] was infra-only this window (`folly::invoke`→`std::invoke`, an OSS-bot hash bump) — no draft-18 protocol commits. See [[quiche-moq]], [[moqtail]].

- **Safari / audio cluster** — **[#2350](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2350)** align the moq-audio capture/encode surface with moq-video (+1666/−1279), **[#2372](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2372)** resume AudioContext from a real user gesture, **[#2369](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2369)** publish video files on Safari, **[#2368](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2368)** render audio at the decoder's real sample rate, **[#2365](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2365)/[#2364](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2364)** worklet/logging fixes — working **fperex**'s live Safari/audio bug sweep (issues **[#2352](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2352)–[#2358](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2358)**: non-48 kHz Opus decoding to noise, relay cancelling a live subscription 5 s after viewer reconnect, `video.captureStream()` unsupported, muted tile still downloading). ## IANA early review of transport-19 — the WGLC punch-list arrives

The window's most consequential WG fact is on the mailing list, not GitHub: **IANA's Amanda Baber posted an early review of [[moq-transport|`draft-ietf-moq-transport-19`]]** (RT ticket #1456083, July 16, [permalink](https://mailarchive.ietf.org/arch/msg/moq/LwEbNcASw4hxFF56BK9-KWjQhAU/)) ahead of IETF 126. It is a concrete pre-Last-Call punch-list:

- The **IANA Considerations section is incomplete** — the draft creates new registries but doesn't fully specify them.
- **Registry-group naming**: clarify whether the new registries belong to an existing group or form a new one (IANA suggests *"Media over QUIC Transport"* / *"Media over QUIC"*).
- **Designated-expert guidance** per RFC 8126 is needed for any Expert-Review / Specification-Required registries.
- **Registry names** should include the protocol identifier to avoid collisions with similarly-named registries elsewhere.
- **Explicit numeric bounds** (e.g. 0–255, 0–65535) must be stated for every registry with numeric limits.
- **Cross-draft coordination**: related drafts ([[moq-loc|loc]], [[moq-secure-objects|secure-objects-01]], [[moq-msf|msf-01]]) request registry values with inconsistent naming and potentially duplicate allocations — the authors need to reconcile them.

The WG reacted the same day: [[alan-frindell|afrind]] opened **[moq-transport #1814](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/issues/1814)** *"IANA early review issues"* and [[will-law|Will Law]] opened **[msf #191](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/issues/191)** *"IANA feedback ahead of IETF #126"*. afrind also merged **[moq-transport #1804](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1804)** *require FETCH to be sent to at least one publisher* (+1/−1). Read against last week's finalized-agenda **"MOQT Update and Path to WGLC"** slot (Frindell, Monday), the review makes the WGLC push tangible: the remaining blockers are enumerated registry/IANA-Considerations items, not open protocol design. See [[moq-transport]].

## moq-dev/moq — a feature day, a second release train, and a contract-drift audit

[[moq-dev|moq-dev/moq]]'s July-16 day (all [[luke-curley|kixelated]] unless noted) pairs genuine new capability with an internal-consistency audit, and cuts **moq-relay v0.13.6** / moq-ffi v0.2.31 / moq-cli v0.8.5 / libmoq v0.3.13 (July 16, the second release train in 48 h after v0.13.5 on July 15):

- **New capability** — **[#2303](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2303)** *adapt encoder bitrate to the congestion-control estimate* (+1026/−47) is the **first CC→encoder link** and the first of last week's roadmap issues ([#2283](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2283)) to ship as code; **[#2293](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2293)** adds **macOS window/app/system-audio capture + device enumeration** (+1478/−240, MERGED after being OPEN July 15) — a new native capture platform; **[#2302](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2302)** reshapes `net` around a caller-driven `(Session, Driver)` pair (+938/−309, merged 00:27 July 17).
- **moq-hls hardening continues** — **[#2299](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2299)** *reshape moq-hls import around per-track ownership + fix HLS conformance* (+1076/−687) and **[#2298](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2298)** release track subscriptions when an export pauses (+251/−58) extend the July-14/15 origin-hardening line.
- **Bindings / net correctness** — **[#2287](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2287)** align portable-API parity across bindings (+841/−52), **[#2307](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2307)** drop `Announced::Restart` (unannounce/announce pair replaces it, +231/−432), **[#2306](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2306)** honor declared track ends + unify abort error codes + dedupe the libmoq native link list, **[#2294](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2294)** accept SNI-less raw QUIC connections, and external contributor **Bleuzen**'s **[#2291](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2291)** allow b-frames in the OBS path.
- **Cross-language contract-drift cleanup** — late July 16 kixelated filed a batch of **17 issues ([#2309](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2309)–[#2325](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2325))** cataloguing where the Rust core, the js/net port, the C bindings, and the draft have silently diverged: **#2309 SUBSCRIBE_END off-by-one** (Rust exclusive vs js/net + draft inclusive), **#2310** JS verify rejecting Rust root-scoped tokens, **#2311** `subscribe_audio` hardcoding Opus, and #2312–#2324 a sweep of bindings/moq-net/moq-ffi/libmoq/js API drift (#2325 stale docs). The **July-17 fixes** begin landing as OPEN PRs: **[#2329](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2329)** align the Rust/JS token contract on sign/verify/authorize (+1089/−462), **[#2328](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2328)** coalesce concurrent group fetches behind a shared Requests queue, **[#2327](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2327)** handle IPv4-mapped peers on a dual-stack socket, and new contributor **Simon Kirsten**'s **[#2326](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2326)** stop racing priority updates against group-frame writes.
- **Still OPEN** — the cross-connection-migration PR **[#2241](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2241)** grew to **+3736/−800** (the connection-resilience theme still in flight). See [[moq-dev]].

## moq-dev/moq — a moq-hls-hardening + release day, and a wide roadmap sketch

[[moq-dev|moq-dev/moq]]'s July-15 day (all [[luke-curley|kixelated]] unless noted) turns the July-14 moq-hls refactor into working origin behavior and lands the first connection-resilience piece:

- **moq-hls → real origin** — **[#2271](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2271)** *honor byte ranges and map changes* (+611/−42, MERGED), **[#2266](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2266)** *reconcile catalog renditions* (+321/−70), **[#2264](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2264)** *account for audio groups in master variants* (+119/−27), and **[#2270](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2270)** moq-mux `cut(end)` as the group boundary (+361/−62) — the byte-range / master-playlist / catalog-reconciliation behavior an HLS gateway needs to serve legacy players from a MoQ ingest. These implement the July-14 moq-hls roadmap issues (#2248–#2253, several closed July 14). Still OPEN: **[#2268](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2268)** cancel HLS subscriptions on pause and **[#2295](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2295)** moq-mux custom catalog tracks.
- **Connection resilience + correctness** — **[#2240](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2240)** *reconnect the moqsink publisher instead of dying on transport death* (+403/−128, MERGED after being OPEN July 14) is the first landed piece of the resilience theme; the bigger **[#2241](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2241)** *migrate subscriptions transparently across connections* stays OPEN and grew to **+3770/−736**. Alongside: **[#2260](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2260)** release per-subscription serve scopes instead of leaking (+118/−12), **[#2262](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2262)** resolve reordered track aliases (+252/−63), **[#2267](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2267)** moq-rtc RTP-clock sync via sender reports (+260/−22), **[#2261](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2261)** SRT egress send-buffer stalls (+71/−3).
- **watch simplification + backend flip** — **[#2288](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2288)** *remove the MSE backend, inline the WebCodecs pipeline* (+187/**−1196**, breaking, MERGED) makes the TS `watch` player **WebCodecs-only**; **[#2285](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2285)** flips moq-native's **default QUIC backend to quinn** (was `noq`, quinn opt-in). A related OPEN pair: **[#2286](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2286)** make net connections caller-driven (+526/−200) and **[#2294](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2294)** accept SNI-less raw QUIC connections.
- **Release train (July 15)** — **[#2200](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2200)** *chore: release* (+214/−142, MERGED) cuts **moq-relay v0.13.5**, moq-ffi v0.2.30, moq-cli v0.8.4, moq-gst v0.2.12 — the first since v0.13.4 (July 12); **[#2290](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2290)** bumped non-Rust package versions.
- **Roadmap issue batch** — kixelated filed **[#2275](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2275)–[#2284](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2284)** + **[#2296](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2296)**: DVR rewind/time-shift (#2275), QUIC multipath bonded contribution (#2276), Hang E2E encryption (#2277), wall-clock latency target (#2278), typed SCTE-35 cues (#2279), caption/subtitle tracks (#2280), broadcast recording/replay (#2281), native echo-cancel/AGC (#2282), CC-bandwidth → encoder (#2283), keyframe request/PLI (#2284), quiche → quinn/noq backend parity (#2296) — the widest forward-looking scope the wiki has logged, spanning media features, transport, and native audio processing.
- **Notable OPEN PRs** — **[#2293](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2293)** macOS window/app/system-audio capture + device enumeration (+1409/−234), **[#2287](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2287)** align portable-API parity across bindings (+830/−33), **[#2289](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2289)** mark hang Catalog/Container `#[non_exhaustive]` (breaking), and external contributor **Bleuzen**'s **[#2291](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2291)** allow b-frames in the OBS path. See [[moq-dev]].

## moqtail — a draft-18 alignment sprint (a second impl converging on the interop target)

[[moqtail]] (maintainers [[zafer-gurel|Zafer Gürel]] + Ali C. Begen), which had sat at **draft-16 only** for months, opened a **draft-18 alignment sprint** July 15–16:

- **[#280](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/280)** bump ALPN to **`moqt-18`** (+4/−4, MERGED), **[#281](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/281)** rename **Extension Headers → Properties** (+456/−465, MERGED — the -19/-18 terminology), **[#283](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/283)** share **draft-18 constants + varint conformance vectors** (+1886/−272, MERGED July 16), **[#219](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/219)** send the relay `wt-protocol` header (+13/−34), and sharmafb's **[#214](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/pull/214)** malformed-track detection for end clients (+121/−37).
- A **~20-issue tracking batch** (TS-6b … TS-20, JS-1/JS-2, MT-1/MT-2, plus **[#282](https://github.com/moqtail/moqtail/issues/282)** LOC property-id renumber) scopes the **moqtail-ts draft-18 API break**: rename Properties, split SUBSCRIBE_NAMESPACE / SUBSCRIBE_TRACKS, PUBLISH_BLOCKED, GREASE, and adoption of the `moqt://` URL scheme.

moqtail's new LOC property-id renumber issue (#282) pairs directly with kixelated's fresh **loc #25** (frame durations/discontinuities) — cross-implementation convergence on the LOC container's property IDs. See [[moqtail]].

## moq-wg/msf — the WG breaks its spec-quiet streak: the Track+Object init-data design lands

For six consecutive update windows the WG produced only comment-level issue/PR churn and no merges. July 14 ends that: [[will-law|Will Law]] merged a three-PR **init-data cluster** into [[moq-msf|moq-wg/msf]], turning a design the MSF page had tracked only as "settled toward" into actual draft text:

- **[#186](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/pull/186)** *"Add new track and Object properties to carry initialization data"* (+50/−20, MERGED) — adds the Track and Object properties that carry init data, retiring the experimental `initTrack` approach (**closes [#153](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/issues/153)** "initTrack does not work"). This is the dual **Track + Object initData** mechanism [[moq-locmaf|LOCMAF]] and the LOCMAF→[[moq-cmsf|CMSF]] packaging plan rely on: a stable Track property for steady-state init plus an Object property for synchronized mid-track changes (period switches, DRM key rotation).
- **[#187](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/pull/187)** *"Clarify track cloning and delta update rules"* (+28/−14, MERGED, **closes [#183](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/issues/183)**) — pins down how tracks clone and how delta updates apply on top of the init-data model.
- **[#189](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/pull/189)** *"Fix typos in initialization data documentation"* (+4/−4, MERGED) — editorial cleanup of the new text.

The cluster closes **#153, #178, #183** (originally raised by [[victor-vasiliev|Vasiliev]] / [[gwendal-simon|gwendalsimon]]) and lands just before MSF's **Friday** slot at Vienna. See [[moq-msf]].

## moq-dev/moq — a ~18-merge consolidation day: PipeWire capture, per-language binding ergonomics, and an AI-review net-API sweep

[[moq-dev|moq-dev/moq]]'s July-13 day (all [[luke-curley|kixelated]] unless noted) is broad rather than deep — polish, platform reach, and net-API hardening on top of the July-10/12 capability jumps:

- **Platform / capture reach** — **[#2238](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2238)** *"feat(moq-video): PipeWire screen capture on Linux"* (+924/−24, MERGED) is the day's largest merge and a genuinely new capture source: MoQ can now ingest a Linux desktop/screen natively. It pairs with still-open Safari hardware-encode **[#2211](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2211)** (fperex, prefer the codecs Safari actually HW-encodes) and a new issue **[#2224](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2224)** (route Firefox video capture through a Worker for native `MediaStreamTrackProcessor`) — the through-line of the week (widen *who and what* can drive MoQ) now extends to capture sources and platforms.
- **Per-language binding ergonomics** — layered on the July-11/12 C-FFI expansion: **[#2236](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2236)** *"feat(swift,ffi)!: ergonomic Swift JSON wrappers; make snapshot mode explicit"* (+365/−86, breaking, MERGED), **[#2230](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2230)** Go raw-frame timestamp writes, **[#2227](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2227)** make `just kt check` compile+test instead of silently skipping, and **[#2214](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2214)** cut a **Python `moq-rs` 0.3.2** release with JSON stream wrappers. The July-11/12 window exposed the raw FFI surface; July 13 makes it ergonomic per language (Swift/Go/Kotlin/Python).
- **AI-review-driven net-API hardening** — a burst of AI-review findings filed as issues **[#2215](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2215)–[#2224](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2224)** and several resolved the same day, landing **[#2234](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2234)** let `finish_at` declare a future exclusive end group (+207/−133, closes AI-review issue [#2219](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2219)), **[#2232](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2232)** remove implicit frame-timestamp helpers (+236/−153), **[#2243](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2243)** type request transports (+83/−45), **[#2235](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2235)** align track-ordering defaults, and **[#2242](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2242)** shorten watch signal input/output groups (+369/−371, breaking). Three AI-review items stay OPEN ([#2217](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2217) moq-ffi announcement lifecycle, [#2216](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2216) js/net typed announcement status, [#2215](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2215) js/net Track.Info ordered default).
- **Fixes / smoke tests** — **[#2231](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2231)** cancel pending audio-worklet loads (fixes the July-12 `AbortError` issue [#2202](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2202), a tidy same-week gap-to-fix), **[#2229](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2229)** avoid redundant URL reconnects, **[#2228](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2228)** scope the video-config effect, **[#2226](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2226)** pace the capture polyfill via `requestVideoFrameCallback`, **[#2225](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2225)** mark the macOS Surface `Sync` so `moq-transcode` compiles, and **[#2239](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2239)** a smoke test exercising browser playback + audio.
- **In-flight (OPEN)** — **[#2241](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2241)** *"net: migrate subscriptions transparently across connections"* (+1206/−238) and **[#2240](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2240)** reconnect the `moq-gst` `moqsink` publisher instead of dying on transport death (+344/−120, superseding bgreenway's #2223 and closing issue [#2212](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2212)) — together an emerging **connection-resilience** theme (survive/transparently recover from transport loss). Also still OPEN: **[#2201](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2201)** advertise session Role in the moq-lite-05 SETUP (updated July 14), **[#2246](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2246)** measure upload bitrate from encoder Stats, **[#2210](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2210)** expose `finish_group()` through the moq-mux import chain (bgreenway).
- **No new release train** in-window (latest is moq-relay v0.13.4, July 12); the only release is the Python `moq-rs` 0.3.2 above. See [[moq-dev]].

## moq-dev/moq — the July-12 evening merge burst: Safari, moq-json, a transcode verb, and v0.13.4

The prior update's check ran early July 12 and captured the just-after-midnight merges (FFI track-info #2177, NVDEC AV1 #2178, announce-ids #2160, raw-frame timestamps #2174, track datagrams #2175). **A larger burst — ~15 PRs — then merged through the July-12 afternoon and evening** (≈14:00–19:20 UTC), consolidating several threads and cutting a release train. All [[luke-curley|kixelated]] unless noted:

- **Safari support** (fperex) — the biggest theme. The earlier single umbrella PR **[#2163](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2163)** was **closed** and reworked into focused, reviewable changes, several MERGED July 12: **[#2198](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2198)** WebTransport datagram-API variants (+208/−17), **[#2192](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2192)** expose the negotiated transport on `Established` (+41/−2), **[#2197](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2197)** surface unsupported-codec errors (+194/−9), **[#2185](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2185)** don't latch the connection off after a Safari `pagehide` (+32/−1), **[#2181](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2181)** align media constraints (+159/−21). Still OPEN: **[#2190](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2190)** Safari hardware encode + worker capture + stable video catalog (+998/−211) and **[#2191](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2191)** advertise 48 kHz Opus + resample non-canonical capture rate (+487/−57). (A batch of interim fperex Safari/watch/publish PRs — #2180/#2183/#2184/#2186/#2187/#2188/#2189 — were opened and **closed unmerged** as the work was split/reworked.)
- **[#2196](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2196)** *"moq-json: split into snapshot/stream modules and expose JSON tracks through moq-ffi/libmoq"* (+2421/−1061, MERGED) — the largest single merge; generic (non-media) JSON tracks are now first-class and callable across the C ABI, reinforcing moq-dev's "generic for any live data" framing.
- **[#2158](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2158)** *"feat(moq-transcode): decode once per source, GPU resize fanout, and a `moq transcode` verb"* (+1470/−239, MERGED) — extends the July-10 GPU-transcode crate with a CLI verb and single-decode → multi-resize fanout. (The two-writer-race fix #2157 was closed unmerged.)
- **Operability + moq-lite**: **[#2172](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2172)** moq-relay Prometheus `/metrics` for node traffic (+537/−1) MERGED — the operability item flagged OPEN a day earlier now lands; **[#2170](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2170)** moq-net + js/net pre-merge API hardening for moq-lite-05 (+910/−615); **[#2132](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2132)** forward the connection transport to the `--auth-api` hook (+143/−27); **[#2199](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2199)** recover the viewer from a same-name republish (+213/−93). Still OPEN: **[#2201](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2201)** advertise session Role in the moq-lite-05 SETUP (+342/−28) and **[#2179](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2179)** cost-based cache-aware routing (moq-lite-06 WIP) + route-cost I-D (+1482/−198).
- **Docs / housekeeping**: **[#2203](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2203)** root-cause + maintainability rules, PR mechanics split into a `CONTRIBUTING.md` (+86/−41); **[#2204](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2204)** / **[#2134](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2134)** version bumps + moq-bot release; dependabot #2194. New issue **[#2202](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2202)** (audio worklet `AbortError` when the AudioContext closes mid-`addModule()`, fperex) OPEN.
- **Release train (July 12)**: **moq-relay v0.13.4**, moq-ffi v0.2.29, moq-cli v0.8.3, libmoq v0.3.12, moq-gst v0.2.11 — the first new release since v0.13.3 (July 9). See [[moq-dev]].

## moq-dev/moq — an FFI/bindings day, and moq-lite-06 becomes a design cycle

[[moq-dev|moq-dev/moq]]'s day (all [[luke-curley|kixelated]] unless noted, nine merges July 11–12) turns from the July-10 media-plane work to the **C-FFI / libmoq embedding surface** — the boundary that lets non-Rust consumers drive the gateway and transcode features:

- **[#2142](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2142)** *"feat(moq-ffi): expose group fetch API"* (+911/−7, Qizot/Jakub Perżyło, MERGED July 11) — the largest FFI merge; group FETCH is now callable across the C ABI.
- **[#2173](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2173)** *"Add origin options and dynamic FFI"* (+657/−29) and **[#2171](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2171)** *"Update libmoq raw track C ABI"* (+448/−40) MERGED July 12 — origin configuration + a reworked raw-track C ABI.
- **[#2177](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2177)** *"Add moq-ffi track info and update"* (+220/−9, MERGED July 12) and **[#2168](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2168)** *"go: catch up wrapper API"* (+460/−9, MERGED July 11) — track-info accessors + the Go wrapper caught up to the expanded surface.
- **[#2161](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2161)** *"feat(moq-native): add quic::Client/quic::Server transport config"* (+512/−131, MERGED July 11) and **[#2146](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2146)** *"refactor: carry moq-video decode timestamps as moq_net::Timestamp"* (+92/−56, MERGED July 11) — transport-config knobs + a decode-timestamp plumbing refactor.
- **[#2178](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2178)** *"moq-video: add NVDEC AV1 decode support"* (+175/−71, MERGED July 12) — a tidy tail on the July-10 GPU pipeline, adding AV1 to the NVDEC hardware-decode path.

Several more FFI refactors stay OPEN (**[#2174](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2174)** raw frame timestamps, **[#2175](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2175)** track datagrams through moq-ffi, **[#2176](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2176)** fold subscribe_media latency into a `MoqMediaConfig` record, plus issues #2166/#2164). The moq-lite-06 line is now a visible **design cycle**, not just a version bump: **[#2179](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2179)** *"cost-based cache-aware routing (moq-lite-06 WIP) + route-cost I-D"* (+1232/−95, OPEN) pairs a routing feature with a vendored route-cost Internet-Draft (continuous with July-10's #2159 draft-vendoring), **[#2160](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2160)** adds typed START/END/RESTART announce ids, and **[#2172](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2172)** *"expose a Prometheus /metrics endpoint for node traffic"* (+501/−1, OPEN) adds relay operability — together a routing-cost + node-metrics theme that reads as moq-dev preparing moq-lite relays for real multi-node CDN deployment. fperex's Safari playback/publish PR **[#2163](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2163)** (+3057/−434) stays OPEN. No new release train in-window (moq-relay v0.13.3 etc. were July 9). See [[moq-dev]].

## moq-dev/moq — a GPU-transcode day, and the WHIP-ingest gap closes

[[moq-dev|moq-dev/moq]]'s day (all [[luke-curley|kixelated]], ~7,000 LOC across six merges July 10) centres on **GPU transcoding** — building a native, ffmpeg-free path from hardware decode through hardware encode:

- **[#2140](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2140)** *"feat: moq-transcode, just-in-time transcoding for hang broadcasts (NVENC-capable)"* (+1567/−20, MERGED July 10) — a **new `moq-transcode` crate** that transcodes Hang broadcasts on demand, using NVENC where available. JIT transcode lets a single ingested broadcast be served in multiple codecs/renditions without a separate encode pass per viewer.
- **[#2145](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2145)** *"feat(moq-video): NVDEC hardware decode, zero-copy NVDEC → NVENC transcode"* (+1392/−166, MERGED July 10) — adds **NVDEC hardware decode** and a **zero-copy NVDEC → NVENC** path so frames never leave GPU memory between decode and re-encode. Together with #2140 this is a complete GPU decode→transcode→encode pipeline.
- **[#2139](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2139)** *"Add RTC H.265 and AV1 ingest bridges"* (+83/−4, MERGED July 10) — the WebRTC gateway's **WHIP ingest now bridges H.265 and AV1**, which WHEP egress already carried. This **closes the July-9 issue [#2133](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2133)** (the ingest/egress codec asymmetry the wiki logged a day earlier) — CLOSED July 10.
- **[#2159](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2159)** *"drafts: move IETF drafts into the monorepo with nix + just"* (+3514/−3, MERGED July 10) — the IETF draft sources (moq-lite, Hang, etc.) are now vendored into the monorepo and built with nix + just, consolidating spec text alongside the code that implements it.
- **[#2156](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2156)** *"Path memory sharing + 32 max parts enforcement"* (+366/−94) — extends the July-8/9 group-memory-pool work with per-path memory sharing and a hard 32-max-parts cap; and **[#2162](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2162)** *"fix(moq-video): make decode Frame/Consumer Send on macOS"* (+23/−9) unblocks the video decode path on macOS.

OPEN in flight: early **`moq-lite-06-wip`** work — **[#2160](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2160)** / **[#2161](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2161)** (announce ids) — the first signal the next moq-lite wire revision (-06) is being drafted after -05 finalized in early July; fperex's Safari hardware-capture batch **[#2163](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2163)** (July 11); Qizot's moq-ffi group-fetch API **[#2142](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2142)**; plus a stack of kixelated planning issues (#2147–#2155) and a moq-ffi `timestampUs=0` regression bug **[#2143](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2143)** (Crypt0XRP0). No new release train in-window (moq-relay v0.13.3 etc. were July 9). See [[moq-dev]].

## cloudflare/moq-rs — PUBLISH message support completes the version track

One day after the July-8 merge burst cleared moq-rs's stalled draft-16 rewrite ([#170](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/170)) and draft-18 request-stream rework ([#178](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/178)), the last large piece of that track lands:

- **[#181](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/181)** *"Publish message support"* (itzmanish, +4049/−624) **MERGED July 9 16:06 UTC** — native PUBLISH-message handling on the draft-16 rewrite baseline. This is the PR that had swelled to +8778 on July 7 as an OPEN attachment to the rewrite; rebased down and merged now that #170 is in.
- Release plumbing followed immediately: **[#186](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/186)** *"chore: release"* (+90/−16) MERGED 16:18 UTC, cutting **`moq-relay-ietf` v0.7.22**, **`moq-pub` v0.9.0** (a new minor, consistent with the PUBLISH feature), `moq-sub` v0.4.11, `moq-clock-ietf` v0.6.17, and `moq-test-client` v0.1.9.

With #170, #178, and now #181 all merged, cloudflare/moq-rs has consolidated its entire draft-16/draft-18 forward track in a two-day window (July 8–9) after weeks of it sitting on long-lived OPEN PRs. See [[moq-rs]], [[moq-transport]].

## cloudflare/moq-rs — a merge burst consolidates the stalled version work

[[moq-rs|cloudflare/moq-rs]] had spent weeks carrying its version work on long-lived OPEN PRs: a `main` still on draft-14, itzmanish's draft-16 rewrite ([#170](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/170), OPEN since May 29), englishm's draft-18 work on the `draft-18-dev` branch, and the AuthHook/C4M auth PRs. On **July 8** the maintainers cleared much of that backlog in one sitting:

- **[#170](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/170)** *"[Rewrite] Draft-16 migration"* (itzmanish, +5456/−3475) **MERGED 04:17 UTC** — the community-led rewrite that had been OPEN since May 29 (itself the successor to the 155-day-old #131) finally lands.
- **[#178](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/178)** *"draft-18: Remove MAX_REQUEST_ID, use bidi streams for requests"* ([[mike-english|englishm-cloudflare]], +1012/−597) **MERGED 04:16 UTC** — the request-ID-removal / requests-on-bidi-streams direction (the WG's draft-18 convergence, cf. transport [Issue #1653](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/issues/1653)), now in.
- **[#169](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/169)** *"docs: add AuthHook trait design proposal"* (englishm, +724/−0) **MERGED 04:19 UTC** — the AuthHook design doc OPEN since May 28 — then **[#183](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/183)** *"Delete docs/AUTH_HOOK_DESIGN.md"* (englishm, −724) **MERGED 13:08 UTC** removes it again the same day (the design presumably superseded now that the concrete AuthHook/C4M impl [#171](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/171) carries the work).
- **[#184](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/184)** *"feat(moq-relay-ietf): expose relay session config"* (itzmanish, +148/−39) **MERGED 13:40 UTC**, plus release-bot cuts **[#182](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/182)** and **[#185](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/185)** (`moq-relay-ietf` **v0.7.21**), and a small README note ([#164](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/164), Renan Dincer).

Still OPEN after the burst: the huge PUBLISH-support PR **[#181](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/181)** (+4049/−624, rebased down from its +8778 July-7 size), the rolling draft-18 vehicle **[#176](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/176)**, the AuthHook/C4M implementation **[#171](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/171)**, the filter framework **[#167](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/167)**, and thexeos's relay interest-loss PR **[#180](https://github.com/cloudflare/moq-rs/pull/180)**. The repo now has both its draft-16 rewrite and its draft-18 request-stream work merged rather than pending — the first real forward motion on moq-rs's `main`/dev tracks the wiki has logged since the June draft-18 landing. See [[moq-rs]], [[moq-transport]].

## MOCHA + TEMPO — a Cisco RTC-application suite over MoQ surfaces

The window's biggest development is not a WG action but the discovery of a **coordinated seven-draft Cisco push** (all submitted July 6, all [[cullen-jennings|Cullen Jennings]] + [[suhas-nandakumar|Suhas Nandakumar]]) that had been flagged only as agenda-request *names* in the July-7 log. They are now confirmed as published individual I-Ds:

- **[[moq-mocha|MOCHA]]** ("MoQ Open Communication & Hosting Architecture") — a **six-part real-time-communication suite** built on [[moq-transport|MoQT]] pub/sub: `draft-jennings-moq-mocha-chat-00` (messaging — each device publishes on its own track in a channel namespace, causal ordering, roster, discovery), plus `-identity`, `-meetings`, `-mls-keying` ([[moq-mls|MLS]] E2E group keying), `-pab` (address book), and `-reactions`. This is the first tracked case of MoQ being used as the substrate for a **full RTC application stack** rather than a media-delivery format — a WebRTC-adjacent vision, with E2E security via MLS that parallels [[moq-secure-objects|secure-objects]]' object-level encryption.
- **[[moq-tempo|TEMPO]]** (`draft-nandakumar-moq-tempo-00`, 14 pp) — "Timing Extension for Media Playout Orchestration": publishers stamp each Object with desired-playout + send times, **relays rewrite the send-time stamp with their own clock** so each subscriber gets a fresh reference from its nearest relay, and subscribers report sync state to a **`PlaySyncServer`** that instructs the publisher to adjust timing when the audience drifts. The synchronized-playout / watch-party problem in native MoQ terms — related to but distinct from [[luke-curley|Luke Curley]]'s age-based [[moq-timestamp|Object Timestamp Extension]].

Both are individual drafts (not WG-adopted or discussed), and are IETF-126 Vienna agenda candidates via the July-7 requests. Also confirmed this pass: the **"MoQ DPoP Proof"** draft Suhas requested time for is the *pre-existing* `draft-nandakumar-moq-generic-dpop-proof-00` (2026-03-01), not a new submission — only the agenda request is new. See [[moq-mocha]], [[moq-tempo]], [[interim-meetings]].

## LOCMAF → CMSF by reference, and QMux for draft-19

- **[[moq-cmsf|CMSF]] registers LOCMAF by reference** — [[tobbe-einarsson|Tobbe]]'s **[cmsf #27](https://github.com/moq-wg/cmsf/pull/27)** *"Register locmaf packaging referencing draft-einarsson-moq-locmaf"* (July 7, +74/−1, OPEN) adds a `locmaf` packaging that **normatively references the standalone [[moq-locmaf|LOCMAF]] draft** rather than folding the format text in — the approach [[will-law|wilaw]] suggested on [Issue #24](https://github.com/moq-wg/cmsf/issues/24). Net effect: LOCMAF **keeps its independent individual-draft path** (contrary to the earlier "fold in and retire" framing); CMSF just signals it in the catalog. Companion: [Eyevinn/locmaf #2](https://github.com/Eyevinn/locmaf/pull/2) bumps `mp4ff` to v0.53.0. See [[moq-cmsf]], [[moq-locmaf]].
- **[[qmux|QMux]] back in focus** — **`draft-ietf-quic-qmux-02`** was published July 6 (QUIC WG, superseding the individual `draft-opik-quic-qmux-01`). In the first technical `#moq` Slack traffic in ~2 weeks, **[[luke-curley|Luke Curley]]** (July 7 21:52 CEST) asks *"qmux-02 or qmux-01 for draft 19?"* — which QMux revision to target as the TCP-fallback substrate for his new [[moq-transport|moqt-19]] support. **Lucas Pardue**: no real difference (correctness enforcement); pick most recent (-02) as baseline, wire is backwards-compatible with -01. **[[alan-frindell|afrind]]**: *"We're not supporting -19 so I guess it doesn't matter, but we can interop -18 on -01."* The MoQ binding `draft-nandakumar-moq-qmux-moqt` is still `-00`; the version choice is over the underlying QMux layer. See [[qmux]], [[moq-dev]].

The interim (agenda: fill-fetch, switch_from, rst/fin, filters, SUBSCRIBE_TRACKS→PUBLISH, concurrent subscriptions, default priority; the "MOQT Issues and PRs" slides + chat log + bluesheets are posted, but no formal minutes yet at check time) resolves the *ready* items into -19: [[mo-zanaty|Mo Zanaty]]'s Range Filters [#1765](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1765) is the one gated Design PR to land, alongside clarification merges [#1698](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1698) (FIN vs RST), [#1788](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1788) (SUBSCRIBE_TRACKS params → PUBLISH), [#1775](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1775) (concurrent subscriptions per Track), [#1613](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1613) (MAX_REQUEST_UPDATES), [#1799](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1799) (enum extensibility) + afrind's [#1802](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1802) (draft-19 release notes) — while the four *contested* Design PRs (fill-fetch [#1673](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1673), SWITCH_FROM [#1674](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1674), Subscription Fill [#1642](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1642), SSTS [#1638](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1638)) all stay OPEN, deferred past -19. On the media side [[tobbe-einarsson|Tobbe]] ships a *major* [[moq-locmaf|LOCMAF -01]] (*"Low Overhead CMAF for Media over QUIC"*) with a new standalone [Eyevinn/locmaf](https://github.com/Eyevinn/locmaf) reference-impl + golden-vector conformance repo and a coordinated **Eyevinn v0.12.0** ([moqlivemock #96/#97](https://github.com/Eyevinn/moqlivemock/pull/96) + [warp-player #150/#152](https://github.com/Eyevinn/warp-player/pull/150)) moving to **LOCMAF v0.3** — the first Slack traffic (July 6 08:15 CEST) in ~14 days. July 7 brings a burst of **IETF-126 agenda requests** (Suhas: MoQ **DPoP Proof** + MoQ **Tempo**; Cullen: MoQ **Mocha** + secure-objects + Auth Design Team; Mo Zanaty replies). Implementations: [[moq-dev|moq-dev/moq]] turns draft-19 support around within hours ([#2106](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2106)) alongside a large codex role-module mirror ([#2091](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2091) +785/−675). The interop runner cuts once (July-6 00:39, **294/83/177/34**, at-target draft-18 **152**) — pass +3, a **fifth** straight cut holding at-target 152 (the cut predates both the interim and -19, so it still targets draft-18). No new [[moq-monthly|MoQ Monthly]] (#2, May 31); no open wiki issues.**

## moq-transport — interim-2026-moq-18 cuts draft-19; Range Filters lands, the contested design PRs defer

The **[[interim-meetings|interim-2026-moq-18]]** ran July 6 16:30 UTC as the pre-Vienna slot focused on open `draft-ietf-moq-transport` issues. Its posted [agenda](https://datatracker.ietf.org/meeting/interim-2026-moq-18/session/moq) queued the design surface the wiki has tracked all cycle — **fill-fetch, switch_from, rst/fin handling, filters, SUBSCRIBE_TRACKS Parameters copied to PUBLISH, multiple concurrent subscriptions per Track, adjustable default publisher priority** — with "Chair opening remarks" + a "MOQT Issues and PRs" deck; materials (slides, chat log, bluesheets) are up, but **no formal written minutes** at check time. The outcome is legible in the same-day GitHub state: **[[moq-transport|`draft-ietf-moq-transport-19`]] was cut July 6** (approved by [[ian-swett|Ian Swett]]; first revision since -18 on May 12), and **seven PRs merged into it**:

- **[#1765](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1765)** *"Add Range Filters"* ([[mo-zanaty|Mo Zanaty]], +181/−24) — **the one gated Design PR to make the -19 cut**; the object/location range-filter work carried since London.
- **[#1698](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1698)** *"Clarify FIN vs RST/STOP_SENDING semantics on request streams"* (afrind, +38/−18).
- **[#1788](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1788)** *"SUBSCRIBE_TRACKS Parameters copied to PUBLISH"* ([[ian-swett|ianswett]], +11/−1) — the fix for [#1660](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/issues/1660).
- **[#1775](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1775)** *"Clarify multiple concurrent subscriptions per Track"* ([[suhas-nandakumar|suhasHere]], +11/−17).
- **[#1613](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1613)** *"Add MAX_REQUEST_UPDATES setup option + TOO_MANY_REQUEST_UPDATES error"* (afrind, +34/−0).
- **[#1799](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1799)** *"Include enumerations in extensible protocol elements"* (afrind).
- **[#1802](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1802)** *"Draft 19 release notes"* (afrind, +34/−0).

The **four contested Design PRs stayed OPEN and are deferred past -19**: fill-fetch [#1673](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1673) (the Cullen/Mo-debated redesign), SWITCH_FROM [#1674](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1674) (+ soft-mode [#1675](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1675)), Subscription Fill [#1642](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1642), and SSTS [#1638](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1638) — alongside [#1770](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1770) (updatable default priority), [#1783](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1783) (safe-ASCII), [#1789](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1789) (impersonation), [#1790](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1790) (PUBLISH params). So -19 is the **ready-clarifications-plus-Range-Filters** revision; the switching / fill-fetch / subscription-fill design cluster remains live work. Range Filters landing immediately generated two new issues July 6: **[#1801](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/issues/1801)** ([[victor-vasiliev|Vasiliev]], *"Reconsider 'OR' functionality in range filters"*) and **[#1800](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/issues/1800)** ([[luke-curley|kixelated]], *"NAMESPACE doesn't allow parameters like PUBLISH_NAMESPACE"*). See [[moq-transport]], [[interim-meetings]].

## The WG draft wave — loc-03, secure-objects-01, and a major privacy-pass-auth-03

Three WG drafts revved on July 6 alongside transport-19:

- **[[moq-privacy-pass|`draft-ietf-moq-privacy-pass-auth-03`]]** — the **most substantial** of the wave, and the first revision since -02. It absorbs the entire July-1 Suhas triage + PR #20 deployment text and rebuilds the MoQT integration: **reverse-flow issuance moves to AUTH REQUEST / AUTH RESPONSE parameters**, **token acquisition over an established connection via `REQUEST_UPDATE`**, the **`PrivateTokenAuth` structures and the auth-scheme registry are removed**, **`TokenChallenge` now rides in AUTH CHALLENGE transport parameters instead of reason phrases** (closing the long-tracked "no clean challenge carrier" problem), a **Privacy Pass codepoint is registered in the MOQT Auth Token Type registry**, retry behavior after challenge errors is specified, and the reverse-flow docs expand to three-phase flows. **[[cullen-jennings|Cullen Jennings]] is now a listed author** (Suhas / Cullen / Thibault). The repo merge behind it: thibmeu's **[PR #21](https://github.com/moq-wg/privacy-pass/pull/21)** *"Transport auth hooks"* (+251/−123, July 6). See [[moq-privacy-pass]].
- **[[moq-loc|`draft-ietf-moq-loc-03`]]** — first LOC revision since -02, resolving the June audio-config gap. Merges: **[#24](https://github.com/moq-wg/loc/pull/24)** *"Add audio config, revise all audio and video properties"* ([[mo-zanaty|Mo Zanaty]], +26/−17) — the answer to [Issue #21](https://github.com/moq-wg/loc/issues/21) (LOC had a Video Config but no audio equivalent for AAC AudioSpecificConfig etc.); and **[#23](https://github.com/moq-wg/loc/pull/23)** *"Fix truncated sentence in MoQ Object Mapping"* (sharmafb). See [[moq-loc]].
- **[[moq-secure-objects|`draft-ietf-moq-secure-objects-01`]]** — first revision since -00 (the long-flagged -01 cut), carrying the June-29 test-vectors appendix (PR #88) + AAD-serialization work into a published draft. No new repo activity fell inside the July 6–7 window (the merges predate it). See [[moq-secure-objects]].

## LOCMAF -01 — Tobbe's major revision, the standalone Eyevinn/locmaf repo, and Eyevinn v0.12.0

The one Slack event (breaking ~14 days of silence) is **[[tobbe-einarsson|Tobbe]]'s July 6 08:15 CEST announcement**: *"LOCMAF is now draft-01… I went through everything to make it consistent, having a canonical unpacking, making it extensible with C2PA boxes like uuid, etc., resulting in a major update… there is a new Github repo with validation tools, golden vectors, etc. at Eyevinn/locmaf. The draft does no longer need any IANA-registration, since all codes are internal, and the packaging is signaled via the CMSF catalog."*

- **[[moq-locmaf|`draft-einarsson-moq-locmaf-01`]]** (July 5 on the datatracker; 41 pp; Torbjörn Einarsson / Eyevinn + Hugo Björs) — the major consistency pass: **canonical reconstruction** (two canonical encoders given the same CMAF input produce **byte-identical** LOCMAF Objects, enabling golden-vector conformance), **generic-box** elements carrying pre-`moof` boxes (`prft`/`emsg`) verbatim + a **raw-boxes** escape hatch (extensible to **C2PA `uuid`** boxes), delta headers via zigzag varints with the first chunk carrying full metadata and later chunks only deltas (down to ~2 bytes), `mdat` unchanged, DRM/encryption metadata preserved. **No IANA actions** — FourCCs are self-describing and field IDs are LOCMAF-governed; packaging is signaled via the [[moq-cmsf|CMSF]] catalog (`packaging: "locmaf"` + `locmafVersion "0.3"`).
- **[Eyevinn/locmaf](https://github.com/Eyevinn/locmaf)** (new repo, created July 3; releases v0.1.0 July 4, **v0.1.1** July 5) — a stdlib-only Go **reference codec** *and* its conformance suite: a byte-pinned **golden-vector** corpus + worked examples (negative CTOs, CENC/cbcs, BMDT re-anchoring, event-only chunks). Layout: `locmaf/` codec, `vi64/` MOQT varints+zigzag, `cmd/locmaf/` CLI, `testdata/` golden vectors, `web/` for the locmaf.dev explainer.
- **Eyevinn v0.12.0** (July 6, all Tobbe) — the codec is **extracted out of moqlivemock into the standalone module**: **[moqlivemock #96](https://github.com/Eyevinn/moqlivemock/pull/96)** *"feat: LOCMAF v0.3 via the Eyevinn/locmaf module"* (+326/−3640 — the big deletion is the in-repo LOCMAF code moving to the module) + **[#97](https://github.com/Eyevinn/moqlivemock/pull/97)** release v0.12.0; **[warp-player #150](https://github.com/Eyevinn/warp-player/pull/150)** *"support LOCMAF packaging version 0.3"* (+3615/−1880) + **[#152](https://github.com/Eyevinn/warp-player/pull/152)** release v0.12.0 (*"LOCMAF v0.3 release cut + Safari MSE latency stabilization"*). See [[moq-locmaf]], [[moqlivemock]], [[warp-player]].

## Privacy-pass — thibmeu's "Deployment considerations" merge lands the reverse-flow key-material text

The day's headline is the first *merged* spec text on the [moq-wg/privacy-pass](https://github.com/moq-wg/privacy-pass) tracker since [[thibault-meunier|Thibault Meunier]]'s June issue burst — **[PR #20](https://github.com/moq-wg/privacy-pass/pull/20)** *"Key considerations"* (thibmeu, created July 5 07:46 UTC, **MERGED July 5 21:42 UTC by [[suhas-nandakumar|Suhas Nandakumar]]**, +44/−5, one file). It adds a new **`# Deployment considerations`** back-section to `draft-ietf-moq-privacy-pass-auth.md` addressing two key-bootstrap problems the draft had left explicitly undefined:

- **Fetching key material for the bootstrap issuer** — the draft still does not define how Clients retrieve issuer configuration or establish a relationship with an attester. It now notes Clients *may* reuse issuers exposed over HTTP (**RFC 9577**), and *should* reduce susceptibility to **partitioning** (which erodes the Privacy Pass privacy guarantee, RFC 9576 §6.2) by relying on consistency **mirrors** (the `draft-ietf-privacypass-consistency-mirror` I-D, multiple vantage points) or transparency mechanisms such as **KEYTRANS** (`draft-ietf-keytrans-architecture`) or **SCITT** (RFC 9943).
- **Fetching key material for the relay *reverse* issuer** — the tie-back to the July-1 triage: for a **relay operating in reverse-issuer mode** (the reverse-flow token exchange Suhas raised in **[Issue #19](https://github.com/moq-wg/privacy-pass/issues/19)**), Clients should similarly retrieve key material — either via a **pre-established relation** (shipped as part of app install / webpage load / device setup) or **on the fly** (HTTP, or a MoQ API). The draft leaves the mechanism undefined but says the same consistency considerations apply.

The PR also reformats the entity list (Client / MoQ Relay / Privacy Pass Issuer / Attester) into a definition list, renames "Change Log" → "Changelog", and adds references to thibmeu's own **`draft-meunier-privacypass-reverse-flow`** plus the mirror/KEYTRANS/SCITT I-Ds. This is the concrete spec-text follow-through on the **July-1 Suhas triage** (#19 reverse-flow, #18 challenge carrier, #16 retry) — the auth design-team work converting from tracker issues into merged draft text, one day out from the interim. No new datatracker revision yet (still -02; the merge is on `main`, not cut into a new -03). See [[moq-privacy-pass]], [[moq-c4m]], [[moq-transport]], [[interim-meetings]].

The [[moqlivemock|Eyevinn]] stack lands a coordinated **joining-FETCH catalog-retrieval** feature across all three repos July 4 (all [[tobbe-einarsson|Tobbe]]): [Eyevinn/moqtransport **v0.9.0**](https://github.com/Eyevinn/moqtransport/releases/tag/v0.9.0) ships ([PR #14](https://github.com/Eyevinn/moqtransport/pull/14) *"resolve joining FETCH on the publisher side (draft-16)"*), and both the Go publisher/subscriber ([moqlivemock PR #95](https://github.com/Eyevinn/moqlivemock/pull/95)) and the TS player ([warp-player PR #149](https://github.com/Eyevinn/warp-player/pull/149)) now *"retrieve catalog via SUBSCRIBE plus relative joining FETCH"* + document a `-catalog-mode` — the first substantive Eyevinn activity the wiki has logged since the June-9 hackathon hardening. [[moq-dev|moq-dev/moq]]'s codex sweep tails off July 4 afternoon: **moq-wasm is backported to `main`** ([#2086](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2086) +727/−29, with wasm-compat fix [#2085](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2085) by ewindisch), the `Once` write-once signal merges ([#2080](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2080)), per-project relay stats land ([#2078](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2078) +363/−109), and the catalog-reservation + role-module themes spawn follow-ons (OPEN [#2091](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2091) mirror `@moq/net` role modules +762/−671, [#2089](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2089)/[#2090](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2090) catalog reservation, [#2092](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2092) ffi lifetimes). A **third external-filed defect** appears: [Issue #2087](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/issues/2087) (pisoj) *"Go: Catalog becomes unavailable after a minute of playing"* — a Go client gets `RESET_STREAM` on the `catalog.json` track after ~1 min unless the broadcast is re-published in a loop, a catalog-lifetime bug in the same family as [msf #188](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/issues/188) / the #2072 catalog-reservation gating. **moq-transport quiet** — no merge or new issue July 4–5 (only afrind's [#1799](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1799) enum-extensibility touched July 5 00:52 UTC, metadata-only); gated Design PRs (#1765/#1638/#1642/#1673/#1674) all stay OPEN pending July 6. ## Eyevinn — joining-FETCH catalog retrieval lands across moqtransport / moqlivemock / warp-player

The day's headline on the impl side is a **coordinated [[tobbe-einarsson|Eyevinn]] feature** shipping across all three repos July 4 16:48 → 17:32 UTC — making a **relative joining FETCH** the mechanism for retrieving the catalog, rather than a plain SUBSCRIBE-and-wait:

- **[Eyevinn/moqtransport **v0.9.0**](https://github.com/Eyevinn/moqtransport/releases/tag/v0.9.0)** (July 4 16:48 UTC) — the transport library's single change is **[PR #14](https://github.com/Eyevinn/moqtransport/pull/14)** *"feat: resolve joining FETCH on the publisher side (draft-16)"*, i.e. the publisher can now satisfy a joining FETCH (a FETCH relative to the current group). This is the library primitive both apps consume.
- **[moqlivemock PR #95](https://github.com/Eyevinn/moqlivemock/pull/95) MERGED** (July 4 17:30 UTC) *"feat: retrieve catalog via relative joining FETCH"* — the Go publisher/subscriber bumps its `moqtransport` dependency to **v0.9.0** and adds a **`-catalog-mode`** flag (documented) selecting how `mlmtest` fetches the catalog track.
- **[warp-player PR #149](https://github.com/Eyevinn/warp-player/pull/149) MERGED** (July 4 17:32 UTC) *"feat: retrieve catalog via SUBSCRIBE plus relative joining FETCH"* — the TS/MSE player mirrors the same mode (SUBSCRIBE to pick up live catalog updates, plus a relative joining FETCH to grab the current catalog object immediately instead of waiting for the next publish).

This is the first June-cycle-substantive Eyevinn activity the wiki has logged since the [[interop-runner|June-9 London-hackathon hardening]] (bounded SETUP / draft-14-downgrade refusal), and it directly addresses the *"how does a subscriber get the current catalog without a full-group wait"* problem that also underlies [[luke-curley|kixelated]]'s [msf #188](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/issues/188). See [[moqlivemock]], [[warp-player]].

## moq-transport — Cullen's review sweep + the fill-fetch design debate on the list

No transport merge or new issue July 3–4, but July 3 saw the most concentrated **editor review activity** since the June-29 merge batch — **Cullen Jennings ("fluffy") swept five open PRs** (~15:04–17:20 UTC), the signature of a pre-deadline push to clear the Design-PR queue ahead of **[[interim-meetings|July 6]]**:

- **[#1674](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1674)** *"Track Switching via the SWITCH_FROM parameter"* (afrind) — **APPROVED** (15:26 UTC); Cullen is fine merging as-is and filing follow-ups, then adds *"this should be rebased onto main"* (15:31).
- **[#1783](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1783)** *"Restrict reason phrase and implementation name to safe ASCII"* (afrind) — **APPROVED** (16:40 UTC) with the pointed *"if we don't do this now … we will be doing it in IETF Last Call"* — the WGLC-hygiene argument.
- **[#1770](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1770)** *"Make Default Publisher Priority an updatable parameter"* ([[michal-hosna|michalhosna]]) — **COMMENTED** (16:32 UTC): residual race-condition concerns remain, but Cullen thinks applications can live with them.
- **[#1789](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1789)** *"Add additional details on preventing impersonation"* ([[suhas-nandakumar|suhasHere]]) — touched (17:20 UTC).
- **[#1673](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1673)** *"Replace Joining FETCH with fill fetch streams"* (afrind) — **CHANGES_REQUESTED** (15:04 UTC), the substantive one (below).

The **fill-fetch redesign** (#1673, a revision of Subscription-Fill #1642: satisfy any subscription range, delivering the pre-Largest-Object portion on a "fill-fetch" unidirectional stream) drew a parallel **"Re: Review of PR1673"** list thread July 3. **Cullen's concerns**: (1) **error delivery** — with a dual SUBSCRIBE + FETCH model there's no clean way to return fill-stream error details, which breaks token-refresh for non-bearer auth; (2) **duplicate object delivery** — objects can arrive twice (FETCH + subscription), and the `FILL_TIMEOUT=0` mitigation is *"poor design"*; (3) **implicit vs explicit** — he disputes auto-cancelling fill-fetches on `forward=0` (use case: join at low-res while back-filling 30 s, then upgrade quality without abandoning the backfill); (4) **nested parameters** — `FILL_PARAMETERS` containing other parameters is confusing when a parameter behaves differently by context; (5) terminology ("already-published", before/after → location-based); (6) he'd rather **keep Joining FETCH** until there's operational experience. **[[mo-zanaty|Mo Zanaty]] concurs** on nesting — *"nested parameters are likely to surprise and confuse people"* — and proposes the spec explicitly state top-level-vs-nested rules, noting **delta-encoding context resets at each nested level** and must be documented. The gated **Design PRs** (Range Filters [#1765](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1765), SSTS [#1638](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1638), Subscription Fill [#1642](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1642), fill-fetch [#1673](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1673), SWITCH_FROM [#1674](https://github.com/moq-wg/moq-transport/pull/1674)) all stay OPEN — the **July-6** gate has not lifted. See [[moq-transport]], [[interim-meetings]].

## MSF — kixelated opens the static-vs-dynamic catalog question

**[[luke-curley|kixelated]] opened [msf Issue #188](https://github.com/moq-wg/msf/issues/188)** *"Static vs dynamic catalogs"* (July 3 20:13 UTC) — a design question surfaced directly by his own [[moq-dev|moq-dev/moq]] gateway work. His fMP4 / FLV / MPEG-TS / HLS **exporters need static tracks** (those containers can't add or remove tracks mid-stream), but **MSF is inherently dynamic** (tracks can be added/removed/modified at any time), and because some publishers initialize tracks *dynamically* there's a **race condition** on the consumer side. He asks MSF for a way to **signal that no more tracks will be added/removed/modified**, so a consumer can abort a "gross" sleep-based wait early instead of guessing when the catalog has settled. This is the spec-side counterpart to the same-window moq-dev fix **[#2072](https://github.com/moq-dev/moq/pull/2072)** *"gate initial catalog publish until reserved tracks resolve"* and the earlier catalog-jitter (#2020) / catalog-root (#2054) codex fixes — the media-gateway breadth generating concrete MSF requirements. No new MSF revision (still -01). See [[moq-msf]], [[moq-dev]].
